Another possible algorithm: Use "convex optimization of log-domain weights for distributions of sums". Let p_i>0. The sum distribution q_k = ∑ p_i p_{k-i}. We can think of q as matrix product. If we choose log p_i = u_i, q_k = ∑ exp(u_i+u_{k-i}). Max log-sum. Objective max log q. Minimize logsumexp ∑_{k} max(log q_k - log T). KKT for minimizing T maybe: at optimum, active q_k = T. Derivatives of log q_k wrt u_i = p? Could do multiplicative updates:
Given q, update p_i to reduce large q? For a normalized distribution, set p_i ∝ exp(-β * sensitivity_i) where sensitivity_i = weighted average of p_{k-i} over active k. This is similar to FISTA but in log domain and multiplicative, maybe avoids simplex projection and better handles nonneg.

Specifically, minimize φ=∑_k softmax-weight q_k or logsumexp(q/T). Gradient wrt p_i = ∑_k w_k ∂q_k/∂p_i = 2∑_k w_k p_{k-i}, with p outside zero. For log p, gradient = p_i times above. So update u_i -= step * p_i * ... but also maintain sum via normalization. Equivalent to p_i <- p_i * exp(-step G_i) normalized. This is mirror descent with entropy. It may exploit scale. We can implement alternating:
- Normalize p sum S.
- Compute weights w_k = softmax(c_k/T) or (c_k/T)^p etc.
- Compute G_i = 2 * convolution? w? We need for each i sum_k w_k p_{k-i}. This is convolution of w with reversed p? For full convolution weights length 2n-1, p length n. G_i = 2 * (w * p_rev?) Similar to earlier gradient. For softmax weights normalized, gradient of ∑ w c? If f=∑? Use smooth function F(c)=∑ e^{β c_k}/β? Gradient = 2*ift(softmax weights * conj(A)). In p-log domain, update u_i = u_i - step * p_i^{-1}? Wait derivative of F wrt p = grad. For mirror with φ(p)=∑ p log p, natural gradient = grad/p. But F nonlinear and not convex in p. Let's focus on full-dim optimization with better algorithm.

Detailed algorithm design:

1. Time management:
   - total budget = 2400 maybe. Provide safety: start = time.monotonic(); deadline = start + budget - 30. If harness uses 2400, budget_s default 2400. We'll maintain returned best.
2. Candidate representation: float64 arrays length n, nonnegative, sum S = sqrt(2n) for target score = maxb.
   - Function `score_array(a)` returns fft_convolve_self(a).max(); maybe if sum not S, evaluate scale-invariant: score = 2n*maxb/sum^2. But keep S.
3. Load `height_sequence_1` if exists. Clip, normalize. If length not one of target lengths? Could interpolate/downsample to target n. But parent length 8192, use as is.

Choosing n:
- If parent length in [4096, 65536], maybe use n=parent length. Parent likely 8192. Could also try n*2 if time. For simplicity main n = len(parent) if 2048<=len<=32768 else 8192. Larger n can improve but evaluation final O(n^2) maybe. Could choose 16384 for more freedom. But parent local for 8192 maybe harder to improve. Starting from parent 8192 likely needed. Could also upscale parent to 16384 by interpolation and optimize. Continuous scale maybe lower. We can attempt main n=8192, then if time and best >target, try n=16384 with upsampled parent and profile initializations, polish. The final best can be from either. Need avoid final np.convolve on huge too slow? We can evaluate internal and only at end. If n=32768 eval final maybe ok but not many.

4. Initializers:
   - parent normalized.
   - reversed parent (same score, but can combine).
   - symmetrized parent with reversed, maybe normalize; also anti-symmetrized? a'=(parent + max?) not.
   - Various generated profiles:
       * uniform.
       * windows (cosine, Gaussian, sine powers) maybe normalized.
       * sinusoidal offset: a_i = max(1 + λ sin(2π m i/n + phase), eps) for m in [1,2,3,5], λ [0.5,0.9].
       * product of window and sinusoid: sin(π x)^p * (1+λ sin...).
       * random positive Fourier: low-frequency coefficients random, exp, normalized.
       * random smooth Gaussian-process? a = exp(sum random sinusoids) normalized.
       * two/three lobe designs? We can generate using random superpositions of Gaussian bumps with positive/negative? Need positivity.
   But time for evaluating many inits maybe small; FISTA refine.

5. Optimizer `soft_descent`:
 We'll implement a general accelerated projected gradient on smoothed objective `F(a) = (1/beta) logsumexp(beta c_k)` or `sum c_k^p` or peak-weight.
 But to improve parent, maybe use fixed high beta and line search, not beta anneal from low. Start with best current and optimize target threshold.

Potential high-dimensional local methods:
A. FISTA-LSE with adaptive step and y feasibility.
B. Direct "peak-weighted" projected gradient with line search (no acceleration) but stronger.
C. Random restarts/mutations.

def convolve_self_fft(a):
    n=len(a)
    size=2*n-1
    fsize=1 << (size-1).bit_length()
    A=fft(a, fsize)
    c=np.real(ifft(A*A))[:size]
    return c, A
```

- `project_simplex(v, S)`: Use parent. Need careful if v contains NaN. Input float array. Implementation:
```
def project_simplex(v, S):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64)
    if np.any(~np.isfinite(v)):
       v = np.where(np.isfinite(v), v, 0.0)
    n=v.size
    x=np.maximum(v,0.0)
    # subtract unsorted algorithm
    # use sort
    u=np.sort(x)[::-1]
    css=np.cumsum(u)
    rho=css - S - np.arange(1,n+1,dtype=float)
    cond = rho <= 0? Wait parent valid = tc <= w where tc=(cumsum-S)/idx. valid if (cumsum[rho-1]-S)/rho <= rho_vals[rho-1]. Equivalent cumsum - S <= rho * w. Use vector.
    theta = max(0,(css[rho]-S)/rho)
    return np.maximum(v-theta,0)
```
Need handle S negative no.
Could use faster projection via `scipy.optimize.optimize`? No.

def normalize_sum(a, target=None):
    a=np.asarray(a,float); clip 0..1000; finite
    s=a.sum(); if s<1e-15: return ones target/n
    if target None: target=np.sqrt(2n)
    return a*target/max(s,1e-300)
```

def make_weights(c, mode, beta, k, power):
   if mode topk:
      k=min(k,len(c))
      idx=np.argpartition(c, -k)[-k:]  # returns last k largest? Need sort within.
      vals=c[idx]
      w=np.power(np.maximum(vals,0), power)  # all positive
      s=w.sum(); if s==0: uniform; else w/=s
      p=np.zeros_like(c); p[idx]=w
   else:
      # multiply by exp(beta*(c - cmax)) ; but if beta high, only max. Add plateau by using c - (cmax - width)
      x=c - c.max()
      x=np.maximum(x, -width) maybe
      e=exp(beta*x)
      p=e/sum
```

Let's refine FISTA implementation for speed and quality.

`renormalize_vector(v, S)`:
```
def safe_renormalize(v, S):
    v = np.maximum(v, 0.0)
    s = v.sum()
    if s <= 1e-15:
       return np.full(v.shape, S/v.size)
    if not np.isfinite(s): return uniform
    return v * (S/s)
```
Use for y. For x from project exact sum.

`run_fista` parameters: `weight_mode`: 'lse' or 'topk', beta, topk, power, step_factor, noise_every.
Pseudo detailed:
```
def run_fista(a_init, n, S, max_time, rng, mode='lse', beta=20.0, topk=512, power=1.5,
              step_factor=1.0, noise_scale=0.005, restart_every=1000):
    a = normalize_sum(a_init, n) * (S/??) normalize target S
    y = a.copy()
    t = 1.0
    start = time.monotonic()
    best = score(a)
    best_a = a.copy()
    last_best = start
    step_factor_cur = step_factor
    it=0
    while time - start < max_time:
       it+=1
       y = safe_renormalize(y,S)
       c = fft_conv_self(y)[0]
       score_y = c.max()
       if score_y < best - 1e-12:
          best=score_y; best_a=y.copy(); last_best=monotonic(); step_factor_cur=min(3, step_factor_cur*1.05)
       # weights
       if mode topk:
          p = topk_weights(c, topk, power)
       else:
          p = lse_weights(c, beta)
       g = grad_from_weights(y,p)
       gn=norm(g)
       if gn < 1e-14: break
       step = step_factor_cur / (max(beta,1.0) * gn)
       # cap step to e.g. 10 * rms / gn? Actually if beta small, step maybe huge. Cap by relative norm:
       rms = np.sqrt(np.dot(y,y)/n)
       max_step = 2.0 * rms / gn  # gives norm change <=2*rms (large)
       step = min(step, max_step)
       x = project_simplex(y - step*g, S)
       # FISTA update
       t_new=0.5*(1+sqrt(1+4t*t))
       y = x + ((t-1)/t_new)*(x-a)
       # keep y feasible by safe normalize
       y = safe_renormalize(y,S)
       a = x
       t = t_new
       # periodically/noise/restart if no improve
       if monotonic - last_best > 120:  # 120s no improve
           # small noise to y and reset momentum
           scale = noise_scale * rms
           if scale > 1e-8:
              y = safe_renormalize(y + rng.normal(0, scale, n), S)
              a = y.copy()
              t=1.0
              step_factor_cur *= 0.7
              # if still no improve >300s, maybe restart from best plus bigger noise or random
           if monotonic - last_best > 300:
              # restart from best_a with noise or random
              base = best_a
              mix = 0.8
              y = safe_renormalize(mix*base + (1-mix)*rng.uniform(0,1,n), S)
              a=y.copy(); t=1.0; step_factor_cur=step_factor
              last_best = monotonic() # avoid immediate
       # Optional: every 5000, evaluate x score? But y after momentum maybe. We track y.
    # final check best_a, maybe also run line search? external
    return best_a,best
```
Issue: We don't evaluate x separately; y after momentum may not be as good. But over iterations, y is a sequence of feasible (renormalized) extrapolations; it may improve. If we restart from best, okay. However, best_a updated from y, not x. FISTA momentum can make y have worse score but gradient direction from y. Standard. Should work.

Could evaluate x every iteration and update best if better at no extra cost if we compute c_x? But we need c_y. We can compute score_x maybe with same A? No. Skip.

But to know current objective for step adaptation, score_y. Good.

Top-k weights function:
```
def topk_weights(c,k,power):
   k=min(max(1,k), len(c))
   idx=np.argpartition(c, -k)[-k:]  # if k==len, argpartition with kth=len-1
   vals=c[idx]
   w=np.power(np.maximum(vals,1e-30), power)
   ws=w.sum()
   if ws<=0: return np.full(len(c),1/len(c))
   p=np.zeros(len(c)); p[idx]=w/ws
   return p
```
For k large e.g. 2048, argpartition O(n). Good.

LSE weights:
```
def lse_weights(c,beta):
   xm=c.max()
   # To focus top, use shift = c - (xm - width), width = max(0.01*xm, 0.05)
   width = max(0.02*xm, 5e-4)
   x = c - (xm - width)
   x = np.where(x>0, x, 0) # may make all zero? at max positive
   e=np.exp(beta*x)
   s=e.sum()
   if s<=0: p uniform
```
If width small, only peaks. If beta moderate, broad. Maybe use x = c; standard softmax.

Maybe better: combine topk and lse: weights = 0.5 topk +0.5 lse. Could do mode 'mix'.

Gradient from weights p length 2n-1. For large n, memory p 16k, fine.

Edge case: if p all zero? no.

      for mult in [1,0.5,0.25,0.125,0.0625]:
          x=project(a - base*mult*g)
          c_x=conv(x); s_x=c_x.max()
          if s_x < best_s: best_s=s_x; best_x=x
      if best_s < score - 1e-12:
          # compute momentum dir = best_x - a
          dir = best_x - a
          # try momentum m in [0,0.2,0.4]? Could test? Just maybe 0.3 if recent improvements
          if it%5==0 and momentum>0:
              y=project(a + momentum*dir, S) # Wait a old; direction from old to new. Heavy-ball new = new + m*(new-old). Use a_new2 = project(best_x + momentum*(best_x - a), S)
              c2=conv(a_new2); s2=...
              if s2 < best_s: best_x=a_new2
          a=best_x
          last_good_base = base*best_mult
      else:
          # no descent, maybe tiny random or reduce
          a = safe_renormalize(a + rng.normal(0, small, n))
      # every 100 iters try exact subgradient? 
```

def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2380):
   start=time.monotonic(); deadline=start+budget_s-20
   rng=default_rng(seed)
   # load parent
   parent = get_parent()
   # choose main_n
   if parent available and len(parent)>=4096 and len(parent)<=16384: main_n=len(parent)
   else: main_n=8192
   S=np.sqrt(2*main_n)
   best_a = normalize parent or uniform
   best_score = score(best_a)
   print
   # generate inits list (name, arr)
   inits = []
   if parent: inits append parent, reversed, sym, maybe scaled.
   inits += generate_profiles(main_n, rng)
   # quick evaluate all inits, sort by score, keep top N (e.g., 10)
   scored=[]
   for arr in inits: a=normalize; s=score; scored append
   sort by s
   # Phase 1: Run FISTA on selected top inits with distinct settings
   # Time allocation: 55% if main n, 20% for alternate n, 25% polish
   t_phase1 = usable*0.55
   settings=[(beta 5 topk 1500 power1), (beta10 topk512 p1), (beta20 lse width), ...]
   for selected_init i settings cycled allocate t_phase1 / count
   run_fista, update global best
   # Phase 2: line search polish best for 20% time
   run_line_search(best, time)
   # Phase 3: alternate length (16384 or main/2) for remaining 15%
   if time >300:
       alt_n = 16384 if main_n==8192 else main_n//2 maybe
       alt_a = resample(best_a to alt_n) or generate
       run_fista and maybe line_search; update
   # final micro line search 5% if time
   # verify evaluate_sequence
   return list(best_a)
```

Resampling/Interpolation for alt length or coarse:
```
def resample_1d(a, n_new):
   n=len(a)
   if n==n_new: return a
   old=np.linspace(0,1,n)
   new=np.linspace(0,1,n_new)
   b=np.interp(new, old, a)
   return b
```

```

Need ensure `basis_to_seq` respects S:
```
def basis_to_seq(theta, n, S, basis):
   z = basis @ theta
   z -= z.max()
   q = np.exp(z) / np.exp(z).sum()
   return q*S
```
If exp underflows? z max subtract, okay. Add guard.

Basis generation top-level:
```
def make_basis(n,D):
   x = np.arange(n, dtype=float)/n
   xs=x-0.5
   cols=[]
   # Fourier: for m=1.. maybe 8: cos, sin
   maxf = min(D//2, 12)
   for m in range(1,maxf+1):
      cols.append(np.cos(2*np.pi*m*x))
      cols.append(np.sin(2*np.pi*m*x))
   # Polynomials (Legendre-like) up to min remaining
   deg=1
   while len(cols)<D:
      cols.append(xs**deg)
      deg+=1
   if len(cols)>D: cols=cols[:D]
   # center columns to reduce numerical? subtract mean? We can center each basis column by its average under uniform? This helps.
   X=np.column_stack(cols)
   X = X - X.mean(axis=0)
   # Scale columns to unit max or std
   for j in range(X.shape[1]):
      s=np.std(X[:,j]); if s>0: X[:,j]/=s
   return X
```
Centering changes softmax invariance but okay. Scaling affects θ scale.

Basis search Adam:
```
def run_basis_search(n,S,max_time,rng,D=24,beta=20,topk=256,sigma=1.0,lr=0.05):
    basis=make_basis(n,D)
    best_a=ones uniform; best_score=inf
    start=monotonic
    # maybe initial θ from random and upsample? We can initialize with random restarts.
    max_restarts = 200
    for r in range(max_restarts):
       if remaining < 3: break
       per = min(5.0, remaining/ max(1,max_restarts-r))
       theta = rng.normal(0, sigma, D)
       # use Adam state
       m=np.zeros(D); v=np.zeros(D)
       t_adam=0
       last_best=monotonic()
       # first step maybe no gradient? 
       while monotonic() - start < max_time and monotonic()-restart_start < per:
           if time short break
           a=basis_to_seq(...)
           c=conv; score=c.max()
           if score<best: best=score; best_a=a.copy(); last_best=...
           if no improve >2s: break restart
           p=mixed_weights(c, k1=16,p1=2, k2=min(512, n), p2=1)
           ga=grad_from_weights(a,p)
           q=a/S
           # gradient theta
           weighted_ga = q*ga
           sumqga = weighted_ga.sum()
           mean_phi = basis @ q
           wmean = basis.T @ weighted_ga
           grad_theta = S*(wmean - mean_phi*sumqga)
           # Adam
           t+=1
           lr_t=lr*np.sqrt(1-beta2^t)/sqrt(1-beta1^t)
           m=...; v=...
           theta = theta - lr_t*m/(sqrt(v)+eps)
           # enforce theta norm not too large to avoid spikes
           norm=np.linalg.norm(theta); if norm>10: theta*=10/norm
       # after restart, if best improved global update
    return best_a,best
```
Need top-level no closures; all okay.

```
def make_weights(c, mode, beta, topk, power, target=None):
    lc=c.size
    if mode == 'target' and target is not None:
        base = max(0.0, target - 0.02)
        x = c - base
        x = np.where(x > 0, x, 0.0)
        if x.sum() > 0:
            xm = x.max()
            if xm > 0:
                x = x / xm
        else:
            xi = c - c.max()
            x = xi - xi.min()
        e = np.exp(beta * x)
        s=e.sum()
        if s>0: return e/s
    if mode == 'topk':
       ...
    if mode == 'lse':
       xm=c.max(); width=max(1e-3, 0.02*xm)
       x=c-(xm-width)
       x=np.where(x>0,x,0)
       e=np.exp(beta*x); return e/s
    if mode == 'mix':
       p1=topk_weights(c, topk, power)
       p2=lse_weights(c, beta)
       return 0.5*p1+0.5*p2
    else standard topk.
```

Projection: We need exact project onto simplex with upper bound? Evaluator caps 1000 but sum small, no cap. We only need nonnegative sum S. Project formula. Use `v` may have negative. Implementation robust:
```
def project_simplex(v,S):
   v=np.asarray(v,float)
   if not finite: v=np.nan_to_num(v,0,0)
   n=v.size
   if S<=0: return zeros
   if v.sum() <= 0: return np.full(n,S/n)
   w=np.sort(v)[::-1]
   css=np.cumsum(w)
   rho=css - S
   # rho_k = sum top k - S. theta_k = rho_k/k. We need largest k with theta_k <= w_k.
   valid = (rho/np.arange(1,n+1)) <= w
   if not any: k=n
   else: k=int(np.max(np.where(valid)[0])+1)
   theta = (css[k-1]-S)/k
   res=np.maximum(v-theta,0)
   # Due numerical, rescale exactly
   s=res.sum()
   if s>0 and abs(s-S)>1e-9*S: res*=S/s
   return res
```
If v has positives, should produce sum S. Good.

`safe_renormalize` uses scaling; may not be exact if negative after clip. It's okay.

```
def generate_profiles(n, rng):
   out=[]
   x=np.arange(n)/n
   # windows
   out.append(np.ones(n))
   out.append(0.5*(1+np.cos(2πx)))
   out.append(0.5*(1-np.cos(2πx)))? nonneg min 0 at 0? Actually okay.
   out.append(np.sin(πx)**2)
   out.append(np.sin(πx)**4)
   out.append(np.exp(-4*(x-0.3)**2/0.05))
   out.append(np.exp(-4*(x-0.7)**2/0.05))
   # multi bump
   for _ in range(12):
      K=rng.integers(2,5)
      pos=rng.uniform(0.05,0.95,K)
      widths=rng.uniform(0.02,0.2,K)
      amps=rng.uniform(0.5,1.0,K)
      v=np.zeros(n)
      for p,w,a in zip(pos,widths,amps):
          v += a*np.exp(-((x-p)**2)/(2*w*w))
      # maybe circular wrap? Add wrap by v = exp distance circular
      # Use circular distance: d = abs(x-p); d=min(d,1-d); exp
   # Fourier positive
   for _ in range(8):
      M=4
      v=rng.uniform(0.2,1.0)
      for m in range(1,M+1):
          amp=rng.uniform(0,0.3)/(m)
          phase=rng.uniform(0,2π)
          v = v + amp*np.cos(2πm x + phase)
      v=np.maximum(v,0.01)
      out.append(v)
   # exponentiated Fourier
   for _ in range(8):
      v=np.zeros(n)
      M=6
      for m in range(1,M+1):
         amp=rng.uniform(-1,1)/(m**0.8)
         phase=rng.uniform(0,2π)
         v += amp*np.cos(2πm x + phase)
      scale=rng.uniform(0.5,3.0)
      v=np.exp(scale*v)
      out.append(v)
   # sigmoid profiles
   for _ in range(4): phi=rng.uniform(0,1); A=rng.uniform(2,8); v=1/(1+np.exp(A*(x-phi))) + 0.1; out.append(v)
   # anti-window: w=sin(πx)^p; a=w*(1+λ sin(2πm x))
   for _ in range(8):
      p=rng.uniform(0.5,3)
      m=int(rng.integers(1,6))
      lam=rng.uniform(0.3,1.0)
      w=np.sin(np.pi*x)**p
      a=w*(1+lam*np.sin(2πm*x))
      a=np.maximum(a,0.0)
      out.append(a)
   return [np.maximum(a,0.0) for a in out] if finite
```
Need ensure arrays finite, size n. Use `clean_array` helper.

Resample:
```
def resample_sequence(a,n_new):
   n=len(a)
   if n==n_new: return a.copy()
   if n==0: return ones
   old=np.linspace(0,1,n)
   new=np.linspace(0,1,n_new)
   return np.interp(new,old,a)
```

Now, run_fista implementation in detail:
```
def run_fista(a_init, n, S, max_time, rng, weight_mode='topk', beta=20.0, topk=1024, power=1.2, step_factor=1.0, target=None, noise_scale=0.005):
    start = time.monotonic()
    a = normalize_sum(a_init, S)
    y = a.copy()
    t = 1.0
    best = score_normalized(a, S?) # if normalize target S, score=c.max
    best_a=a.copy()
    last_imp = start
    sf = step_factor
    sf_min=0.05; sf_max=8.0
    it=0
    while time.monotonic() < min(start+max_time, start+max_time?) and time.monotonic() < deadline? We'll pass max_time: start+max_time.
       it +=1
       y = safe_renormalize(y,S)
       c = convolve_self_fft(y)[0]
       score_y=float(c.max())
       if score_y < best - 1e-12:
           best=score_y; best_a=y.copy(); last_imp=time.monotonic(); sf=min(sf_max, sf*1.05)
       elif score_y < best: maybe not.
       if it % 200 == 0:
           # evaluate x? x=a current feasible; but y best maybe. c_a? Could compute? skip.
       p=make_weights(c, weight_mode, beta, topk, power, target)
       g=gradient_from_weights(y,p)
       gn=np.linalg.norm(g)
       if gn < 1e-14: break
       rms=np.sqrt(np.dot(y,y)/n)
       beta_eff=max(1.0, beta if weight_mode!='topk' else max(5.0,beta))
       step=sf/(beta_eff*gn)
       max_step=5.0*rms/gn
       if step>max_step: step=max_step
       x=project_simplex(y - step*g, S)
       # FISTA
       t_new=0.5*(1.0+np.sqrt(1.0+4.0*t*t))
       y = x + ((t-1.0)/t_new)*(x-a)
       # ensure y finite and sum S
       if not np.all(np.isfinite(y)):
           y = safe_renormalize(np.ones(n),S)
       y = safe_renormalize(y,S)
       a = x
       t = t_new
       # noise/restart if no improve
       now=time.monotonic()
       if now - last_imp > 90.0:
           # small noise, reset momentum
           sig = noise_scale * max(rms,1e-6)
           y = safe_renormalize(y + rng.normal(0, sig, n), S)
           a = y.copy()
           t=1.0
           sf = max(sf_min, sf*0.75)
           # if still no improve after 240s, mix with random or best
           if now - last_imp > 250.0:
               mix = 0.6
               rnd = safe_renormalize(rng.uniform(0,1,n), S)
               y = safe_renormalize(mix*best_a + (1-mix)*rnd, S)
               a=y.copy(); t=1.0; sf=step_factor; last_imp=now
       if now - last_imp > 90.0 and it % 1000 == 0:
           # every 1000 if stuck, change weight mode? We can't easily; maybe add noise
```
Need careful: `last_imp` not updated when mix? We set to now to avoid immediate. But if mix doesn't improve, last_imp remains now, so next check after 90s. Good.
But if `last_imp = now` after no-improve restart, it delays detection of improvement? It may take 90s to restart again. Fine.

for cand in (best_a,a,y): sc=score... update

Line search implementation:
```
def run_line_search(a_init, n,S, max_time, rng, weight_mode='target', beta=8, topk=512, power=1.5, target=1.5027, mom=0.25):
   a=normalize; best=score; best_a; last=monotonic; step_mult=1.0; prev_dir=None; it=0
   # trial mults list as constant tuple? top-level variable? Can define in function (no closure) okay. Use tuple.
   trial_mults = (1.0,0.5,0.25,0.125,0.0625)
   while time ...:
      c=conv(a); score=c.max(); update
      target_use = min(best, target) if target is not None else best
      p=make_weights(c, weight_mode, beta, topk, power, target_use)
      g=grad(a,p); gn=norm
      if gn<1e-14: break
      base=1.0/gn
      step=base*step_mult
      best_x=a; best_s=score; best_mult=0.0
      for m in trial_mults:
          x=project_simplex(a - step*m*g, S)
          c_x=conv(x); s_x=c_x.max()
          if s_x < best_s - 1e-13:
             best_s=s_x; best_x=x; best_mult=m
      if best_s < score - 1e-12:
          # momentum test
          if it > 20 and mom > 0:
             # direction = best_x - a_old (a old before update)
             diff = best_x - a
             x2=project_simplex(best_x + 0.3*diff, S)
             c2=conv(x2); s2=c2.max()
             if s2 < best_s - 1e-13:
                 best_x=x2; best_s=s2
          a=best_x
          step_mult = min(4.0, max(0.05, step_mult*best_mult*1.05)) # maybe next step same absolute? If best_mult=0.5, step_mult halves.
          last=monotonic
      else:
          # no descent. Try small random or accept best_x if lower score? best_s == score.
          # reduce step and move to best_x if it is not a (could be same trial with slightly lower due numeric)
          if best_x is not a and best_s < score:
              a=best_x
          else:
              # perturb
              rms=np.sqrt(np.dot(a,a)/n)
              sig=0.002*rms
              a=safe_renormalize(a + rng.normal(0, sig, n), S)
          step_mult *= 0.5
          it_no? 
      if time - last > 120:
          # larger perturbation or restart from best
          sig=0.01*rms; a=safe_renormalize(a + rng.normal...); step_mult=1.0
      it+=1
   final eval
```

Basis search implementation:
```
def run_basis_search(n,S,max_time,rng,D=20,beta=20,topk=256,target=None,restarts=50):
   if n < 64: return None
   basis=make_basis(n,D)
   best_a=ones*S/n; best=score
   start=monotonic; end=start+max_time
   r=0
   while r<restarts and monotonic()<end-2:
      per=max(2.0, min(10.0, (end-mono)/max(1,restarts-r)))
      theta=rng.normal(0,1.0,D)
      # maybe one theta from current best? if best_a from coarse previous? skip
      m=np.zeros(D); v=np.zeros(D); t_adv=0; last=mono(); restart_start=mono()
      while monotonic()<end and monotonic()-restart_start<per:
         if monotonic()<end...
         a=basis_to_seq(theta,basis,n,S)
         c=conv(a); score=c.max(); update best; last=...
         if mono()-last > 3.0: break
         target_use = min(best, target) if target is not None else best
         # weights: mix target and topk
         p = make_weights(c, 'mix', beta, min(128,2n), 1.5, target_use)
         ga = grad(a,p)
         q = a/S
         weighted = q*ga
         sumqga = weighted.sum()
         mean_phi = basis.T @ q
         wmean = basis.T @ weighted
         grad_theta = S*(wmean - mean_phi*sumqga)
         # if finite
         t_adv +=1
         lr=0.05
         # Adam
         m = 0.9*m + 0.1*grad
         v = 0.999*v + 0.001*grad*grad
         theta -= lr * m/(sqrt(v)+1e-8)
         norm=norm(theta); if norm>8: theta*=8/norm
      r+=1
   return best_a,best
```

Safe_renormalize:
```
def safe_renormalize(v,S):
   v=np.asarray(v,float)
   v=np.where(np.isfinite(v), v, 0.0)
   v=np.maximum(v,0.0)
   s=v.sum()
   if s<=1e-15:
       return np.full(v.shape, S/v.size)
   return v*(S/s)
```

`clean_sequence`:
```
def clean_sequence(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    if a.size==0: return np.array([1.0])
    a=np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a=np.clip(a,0.0,1000.0)
    return a
```

`normalize_sum`:
```
def normalize_sum(a,S=None):
    a=clean_sequence(a); n=a.size
    if S is None: S=np.sqrt(2.0*n)
    s=a.sum()
    if s<=1e-15:
       return np.full(n,S/n,dtype=np.float64)
    return a*(S/s)
```

`project_simplex` details: Need if v all negative, uniform. But if v has some positives but sum less? formula works. Use finite.
```
def project_simplex(v,S):
    v=np.asarray(v,dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    n=v.size
    if n==0: return v
    v=np.where(np.isfinite(v), v, 0.0)
    if S<=0: return np.zeros(n)
    # If sum of positive parts <=? if v sum <=0 -> uniform
    if v.sum() <= 0.0:
        return np.full(n,S/n)
    w=np.sort(v)[::-1]
    css=np.cumsum(w)
    idx=np.arange(1,n+1,dtype=np.float64)
    theta_vals=(css-S)/idx
    valid=theta_vals <= w
    if not np.any(valid):
       k=n
       theta=(css[-1]-S)/float(n)
    else:
       k=int(np.max(np.where(valid)[0])+1)
       theta=(css[k-1]-S)/float(k)
    res=np.maximum(v-theta,0.0)
    s=res.sum()
    if s<=1e-15:
       return np.full(n,S/n)
    if abs(s-S) > 1e-7*max(1.0,abs(S)):
       res=res*(S/s)
    return res
```
If theta large negative? If v sum < S, projection res sum maybe S? Should.

`fft_conv_self`:
```
def fft_conv_self(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64)
    n=a.size
    size=2*n-1
    fsize=1 << (size-1).bit_length()
    A=fft(a,fsize)
    c=np.real(ifft(A*A))[:size]
    c=np.where(c>0.0,c,0.0)
    return c
```
For n=1, size=1, fsize=1? `(size-1).bit_length()` =0.bit_length()=0 =>1<<0=1. OK. But timeline n large.

`gradient_from_weights`:
```
def gradient_from_weights(a,p):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64)
    p=np.asarray(p,dtype=np.float64)
    n=a.size
    if p.size != 2*n-1:
       # adjust
       if p.size < 2*n-1:
          p2=np.zeros(2*n-1); p2[:p.size]=p; p=p2
       else: p=p[:2*n-1]
    need = 3*n-2
    fsize=1 << (need-1).bit_length()
    A=fft(a,fsize)
    P=fft(p,fsize)
    g=np.real(ifft(P*np.conj(A)))[:n]
    return 2.0*g
```
Check need: len(a)+len(p)-1 =3n-2, to avoid wrap need fsize >= that. `need=3*n-2`; fsize=1 << (need-1).bit_length(). Good.

`topk_indices`:
```
def topk_indices(c,k):
    m=c.size
    k=int(k)
    if k<=0: return np.empty(0,dtype=int)
    if k>=m: return np.arange(m)
    return np.argpartition(c, m-k)[-k:]
```
`topk_weights`:
```
def topk_weights(c,k,power):
    idx=topk_indices(c,k)
    m=c.size
    if idx.size==0: return np.full(m,1.0/m)
    vals=c[idx]
    w=np.power(np.maximum(vals,1e-30), power)
    ws=w.sum()
    if ws<=0 or not finite: return np.full(m,1.0/m)
    p=np.zeros(m); p[idx]=w/ws; return p
```
Potentially if p has large max, okay.

`lse_weights`:
```
def lse_weights(c,beta,width_frac=0.05):
    m=c.size
    xm=c.max()
    width=max(1e-12, width_frac*xm)
    x=c-(xm-width)
    x=np.where(x>0.0,x,0.0)
    xs=x.max()
    if xs<=1e-15:
       # just max lag
       p=np.zeros(m); p[np.argmax(c)]=1.0; return p
    x=x/xs
    e=np.exp(beta*x)
    s=e.sum()
    if s<=0: p... else p=e/s
    return p
```
If beta moderate.

`target_weights`:
```
def target_weights(c,beta,target):
    m=c.size
    base = target - 0.02
    if base < 0: base=0
    x=c-base
    x=np.where(x>0.0,x,0.0)
    xs=x.max()
    if xs<=1e-12:
       return lse_weights(c, max(beta,10.0))
    x=x/xs
    e=np.exp(beta*x)
    # add tiny floor to include lower peaks? maybe e+=1e-12
    s=e.sum()
    if s<=0: ...
    return e/s
```
If target less than min c, x all positive, normalized; okay.

`make_weights`:
```
def make_weights(c, mode, beta, topk, power, target):
    m=c.size
    beta=float(beta)
    if mode == 'target':
       return target_weights(c, beta, target)
    if mode == 'lse':
       return lse_weights(c,beta)
    if mode == 'topk':
       return topk_weights(c, topk, power)
    if mode == 'mix':
       p1=topk_weights(c, topk, power)
       p2=lse_weights(c, beta)
       p=0.5*p1+0.5*p2
       return p/p.sum()
    return lse_weights(c,beta)
```
If target None in target mode? Use target=c.max()? In callers target_use not None. But for safety:
```
if mode=='target' and target is None: return lse_weights...
```

`run_fista` code with settings. Need avoid modifying target? We'll compute target_use each iter.
```
def run_fista(a_init,n,S,max_time,rng,weight_mode='topk',beta=20.0,topk=1024,power=1.2,step_factor=1.0,target=None,noise_scale=0.005):
    start=time.monotonic(); end=start+max_time
    a=normalize_sum(a_init,S)
    y=a.copy(); t=1.0
    c=fft_conv_self(a); best=float(c.max()); best_a=a.copy()
    last_imp=start; sf=float(step_factor); sf_min=0.05; sf_max=8.0
    bad=0; prev_score=np.inf; it=0
    while time.monotonic() < end:
       it+=1
       now=time.monotonic()
       if it % 1000 == 0: print? maybe not.
       y=safe_renormalize(y,S)
       c=fft_conv_self(y)
       score_y=float(c.max())
       if score_y < best - 1e-12:
          best=score_y; best_a=y.copy(); last_imp=now; sf=min(sf_max, sf*1.05); bad=0
       else:
          if score_y > prev_score + 1e-10:
             bad += 1
          else:
             bad = max(0, bad-1)
          if bad > 20:
             sf=max(sf_min, sf*0.85); bad=0
       prev_score=score_y
       target_use = best if target is None else min(best, float(target))
       p=make_weights(c, weight_mode, beta, topk, power, target_use)
       g=gradient_from_weights(y,p)
       gn=float(np.linalg.norm(g))
       if gn < 1e-14:
          break
       rms=float(np.sqrt(np.dot(y,y)/n))
       beta_eff=max(1.0,beta)
       if weight_mode == 'topk': beta_eff=max(5.0,beta)
       step=sf/(beta_eff*gn)
       max_step=5.0*rms/max(gn,1e-15)
       if step > max_step: step=max_step
       x=project_simplex(y - step*g, S)
       t_new=0.5*(1.0+np.sqrt(1.0+4.0*t*t))
       y = x + ((t-1.0)/t_new)*(x-a)
       y=safe_renormalize(y,S)
       a=x
       t=t_new
       now=time.monotonic()
       gap=now-last_imp
       if gap > 60.0:  # 60s no improve
           sig=max(1e-6, noise_scale*rms)
           y=safe_renormalize(y + rng.normal(0.0, sig, n), S)
           a=y.copy(); t=1.0
           sf=max(sf_min, sf*0.8)
           if gap > 200.0:
               rnd=safe_renormalize(rng.uniform(0.0,1.0,n), S)
               y=safe_renormalize(0.65*best_a + 0.35*rnd, S)
               a=y.copy(); t=1.0; sf=float(step_factor); last_imp=now
       if it % 2000 == 0:
           # evaluate a maybe
           ca=fft_conv_self(a); sca=float(ca.max())
           if sca < best - 1e-12:
               best=sca; best_a=a.copy(); last_imp=now
    # final checks
    for cand in (best_a,a,y):
       if cand is None: continue
       sc=sequence_score_from_sum(cand,S?)
```
Need sequence_score_from_sum that assumes sum S? Use `fft_conv_self`. Define:

def score_sum_normalized(a,S): c=fft_conv_self(a); return float(c.max())
```

    final_policy = np.maximum(best_a,0); s=final_policy.sum(); if s>0: ...
```

`run_line_search` final code:
```
def run_line_search(a_init,n,S,max_time,rng,weight_mode='target',beta=8.0,topk=512,power=1.5,target=None,momentum=0.2):
    start=time.monotonic(); end=start+max_time
    a=normalize_sum(a_init,S)
    c=fft_conv_self(a); best=float(c.max()); best_a=a.copy()
    step_mult=1.0; old_best=best; last_improve=start; it=0
    trials=(1.0,0.5,0.25,0.125)
    while time.monotonic()<end:
        it+=1
        c=fft_conv_self(a); score=float(c.max())
        if score < best - 1e-12:
            best=score; best_a=a.copy(); last_improve=time.monotonic()
        target_use = best if target is None else min(best, float(target))
        p=make_weights(c, weight_mode, beta, topk, power, target_use)
        g=gradient_from_weights(a,p)
        gn=np.linalg.norm(g)
        if gn < 1e-14: break
        base_step=1.0/gn
        step=base_step*step_mult
        best_x=a; best_s=score; best_m=0.0
        for m in trials:
            x=project_simplex(a - step*m*g, S)
            cx=fft_conv_self(x); sx=float(cx.max())
            if sx < best_s - 1e-14:
                best_s=sx; best_x=x; best_m=m
        old_a=a
        if best_s < score - 1e-12:
            # momentum optional
            if momentum > 0 and it % 5 == 0:
                diff=best_x-old_a
                x2=project_simplex(best_x + momentum*diff, S)
                cx2=fft_conv_self(x2); sx2=float(cx2.max())
                if sx2 < best_s - 1e-14:
                    best_x=x2; best_s=sx2
            a=best_x
            # update step_mult: keep absolute step near accepted step = base_old*best_m. Since base changes, scale step_mult by best_m.
            step_mult = min(4.0, max(0.05, step_mult*best_m*1.05))
        else:
            # no descent; if best_x is slightly better due numerical accept?
            if best_x is not a and best_s < score:
               a=best_x
            else:
               rms=np.sqrt(np.dot(a,a)/n)
               sig=0.002*rms
               a=safe_renormalize(a + rng.normal(0.0, sig, n), S)
            step_mult=max(0.02, step_mult*0.5)
        gap=time.monotonic()-last_improve
        if gap > 90.0:
            rms=np.sqrt(np.dot(a,a)/n)
            sig=0.01*rms
            a=safe_renormalize(0.8*best_a + 0.2*rng.uniform(0,1,n)? need renorm) # safer: mix with random
            step_mult=1.0
            last_improve=time.monotonic()
    final evaluate candidates
    return best_a,best
```
In no descent mix with random: `rnd=safe_renormalize(rng.uniform(0,1,n),S); a=safe_renormalize(0.8*best_a +0.2*rnd,S)`. Good.
Potential problem: `a` always feasible; after momentum x2 feasible.

`make_basis` uses D. We ensure D>0.
```
def make_basis(n,D):
    x=(np.arange(n)+0.5)/n
    xs=x-0.5
    cols=[]
    maxf=min(D//2, 12)
    for m in range(1,maxf+1): cols cos/sin
    deg=1
    while len(cols)<D:
       cols.append(xs**deg); deg+=1
    if len(cols)<D: pad zeros? not needed if D small? If n? D up to 24, maxf 12 gives 24, okay. If D smaller, enough? If D=1, maxf=0, deg1 -> one. Good.
    X=np.column_stack(cols)
    # center and scale
    means=X.mean(axis=0)
    X=X-means
    for j in range(X.shape[1]):
       s=np.std(X[:,j])
       if s>1e-8: X[:,j]=X[:,j]/s
    return X
```
If D passed >? Ensure at least 1.

`basis_to_seq`:
```
def basis_to_seq(theta,basis,n,S):
    z=theta @ basis? theta shape D, basis n,D; use basis @ theta
    z-=z.max()
    e=np.exp(z)
    s=e.sum()
    if s<=0 or not finite: q=np.full(n,1/n)
    else: q=e/s
    return q*S
```

```

def generate_profiles(n,rng):
  out=[]

       v+=amps[j]*np.exp(-(d*d)/(2*widths[j]*widths[j]))

```

```
def run_basis_search(n,S,max_time,rng,D=16,beta=12.0,topk=256,target=None,restarts=60):
    if n < 32 or max_time <=0: return None, np.inf
    basis=make_basis(n,D)
    a0=np.full(n,S/n); best=sequence_score(a0); best_a=a0.copy()
    start=time.monotonic(); end=start+max_time
    r=0
    while r < restarts:
       remaining=end-time.monotonic()
       if remaining < 2.0: break
       per=min(8.0, max(2.0, remaining / max(1, restarts-r)))
       if per < 1.0: per=1.0
       if end - time.monotonic() < per: per=max(1.0, end-mono())
       theta=rng.normal(0.0,1.0,D)
       mom=np.zeros(D); var=np.zeros(D); t_adv=0
       restart_start=time.monotonic(); last_best=restart_start
       while time.monotonic() < end and time.monotonic() - restart_start < per:
           theta_norm=float(np.linalg.norm(theta))
           if theta_norm > 8.0: theta = theta*(8.0/theta_norm)
           a=basis_to_seq(theta,basis,n,S)
           c=fft_conv_self(a); score=float(c.max())
           if score < best - 1e-12:
               best=score; best_a=a.copy(); last_best=time.monotonic()
           if time.monotonic() - last_best > 3.0:
               break
           target_use = best if target is None else min(best, float(target))
           p=make_weights(c,'mix',beta,min(128,2*n-1),1.5,target_use)
           ga=gradient_from_weights(a,p)
           q=a/S
           weighted=q*ga
           sumqga=float(np.sum(weighted))
           mean_phi=basis.T @ q
           wmean=basis.T @ weighted
           grad_theta=S*(wmean - mean_phi*sumqga)
           if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad_theta)):
               break
           t_adv += 1
           lr=0.05
           mom = 0.9*mom + 0.1*grad_theta
           var = 0.999*var + 0.001*grad_theta*grad_theta
           theta = theta - lr*mom/(np.sqrt(var)+1e-8)
       r += 1
    return best_a, best
```

```python
"""
Multi-stage search for non-negative sequences minimizing
    2*n*max(conv(a,a)) / (sum(a))^2.

The method combines:
- FFT-based full self-convolution and smoothed/top-peak gradients.
- Accelerated projected local search with adaptive step sizes and restarts.
- Exact line-search polishing on the feasible simplex.
- Low-dimensional softmax/Fourier basis coarse construction.
- Diverse analytic initializations and an optional second length.
"""

import time
import numpy as np

try:
    from scipy.fft import fft, ifft
except Exception:
    from numpy.fft import fft, ifft


TARGET_UPPER = 1.5027


def clean_sequence(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    return a


def normalize_sum(a, S=None):
    a = clean_sequence(a)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        a = np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)
        n = 1
    if S is None:
        S = np.sqrt(2.0 * n)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-15:
        return np.full(n, max(S / n, 1e-12), dtype=np.float64)
    return a * float(S) / s


def safe_renormalize(v, S):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    v = np.where(np.isfinite(v), v, 0.0)
    v = np.maximum(v, 0.0)
    s = float(v.sum())
    if v.size == 0:
        return v
    if s <= 1e-15:
        return np.full(v.size, max(S / float(v.size), 1e-12), dtype=np.float64)
    return v * (float(S) / s)


def project_simplex(v, S):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    n = v.size
    if n == 0:
        return v
    v = np.where(np.isfinite(v), v, 0.0)
    if S <= 0.0:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if float(v.sum()) <= 0.0:
        return np.full(n, float(S) / n, dtype=np.float64)

    w = np.sort(v)[::-1]
    css = np.cumsum(w)
    idx = np.arange(1, n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    theta_vals = (css - S) / idx
    valid = theta_vals <= w

    if not np.any(valid):
        k = n
        theta = (css[-1] - S) / float(n)
    else:
        k = int(np.max(np.where(valid)[0]) + 1)
        theta = (css[k - 1] - S) / float(k)

    res = np.maximum(v - theta, 0.0)
    s = float(res.sum())
    if s <= 1e-15:
        return np.full(n, float(S) / n, dtype=np.float64)
    if abs(s - S) > 1e-7 * max(1.0, abs(S)):
        res = res * (float(S) / s)
    return res


def fft_conv_self(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([0.0], dtype=np.float64)
    size = 2 * n - 1
    fsize = 1 << max(1, (size - 1).bit_length())
    A = fft(a, fsize)
    c = np.real(ifft(A * A))[:size]
    c = np.where(c > 0.0, c, 0.0)
    return c


def sequence_score(a):
    a = clean_sequence(a)
    n = a.size
    s = float(a.sum())
    if n == 0 or s <= 1e-12:
        return np.inf
    c = fft_conv_self(a)
    mc = float(np.max(c))
    return float(2.0 * n * mc / (s * s))


def topk_indices(c, k):
    m = c.size
    k = int(k)
    if k <= 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=int)
    if k >= m:
        return np.arange(m, dtype=int)
    return np.argpartition(c, m - k)[-k:]


def topk_weights(c, k, power):
    m = c.size
    idx = topk_indices(c, k)
    if idx.size == 0:
        return np.full(m, 1.0 / float(m), dtype=np.float64)
    vals = c[idx]
    w = np.power(np.maximum(vals, 1e-30), float(power))
    ws = float(w.sum())
    if ws <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(ws):
        return np.full(m, 1.0 / float(m), dtype=np.float64)
    p = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)
    p[idx] = w / ws
    return p


def lse_weights(c, beta, width_frac=0.05):
    m = c.size
    xm = float(c.max())
    width = max(1e-12, float(width_frac) * xm)
    x = c - (xm - width)
    x = np.where(x > 0.0, x, 0.0)
    xs = float(x.max())
    if xs <= 1e-14:
        p = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)
        p[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
        return p
    x = x / xs
    e = np.exp(float(beta) * x)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
        p = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)
        p[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
        return p
    return e / s


def target_weights(c, beta, target):
    m = c.size
    target = float(target)
    base = max(0.0, target - 0.02)
    x = c - base
    x = np.where(x > 0.0, x, 0.0)
    xs = float(x.max())
    if xs <= 1e-12:
        return lse_weights(c, max(float(beta), 10.0))
    x = x / xs
    e = np.exp(float(beta) * x)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
        p = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)
        p[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
        return p
    return e / s


def make_weights(c, mode, beta, topk, power, target):
    beta = float(beta)
    if mode == "target":
        if target is None:
            return lse_weights(c, max(beta, 20.0))
        return target_weights(c, beta, target)
    if mode == "topk":
        return topk_weights(c, int(topk), float(power))
    if mode == "lse":
        return lse_weights(c, beta)
    if mode == "mix":
        p1 = topk_weights(c, int(topk), float(power))
        p2 = lse_weights(c, beta)
        p = 0.5 * p1 + 0.5 * p2
        s = float(p.sum())
        if s <= 1e-300:
            return lse_weights(c, beta)
        return p / s
    return lse_weights(c, max(beta, 1.0))


def gradient_from_weights(a, p):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return p

    need_len = 2 * n - 1
    if p.size != need_len:
        tmp = np.zeros(need_len, dtype=np.float64)
        m = min(p.size, need_len)
        tmp[:m] = p[:m]
        p = tmp

    need = 3 * n - 2
    fsize = 1 << max(1, (need - 1).bit_length())
    A = fft(a, fsize)
    P = fft(p, fsize)
    g = np.real(ifft(P * np.conj(A)))[:n]
    return 2.0 * g


def run_fista(a_init, n, S, max_time, rng,
              weight_mode="topk", beta=20.0, topk=1024, power=1.2,
              step_factor=1.0, target=None, noise_scale=0.005):
    n = int(n)
    S = float(S)
    max_time = float(max_time)
    if max_time <= 0.0 or n <= 0:
        a = normalize_sum(a_init, S)
        return a, sequence_score(a)

    start = time.monotonic()
    end = start + max_time

    a = normalize_sum(a_init, S)
    y = a.copy()
    t = 1.0

    c0 = fft_conv_self(a)
    best = float(c0.max())
    best_a = a.copy()
    last_imp = start

    sf = float(step_factor)
    sf_min = 0.05
    sf_max = 8.0
    bad = 0
    prev_score = np.inf
    it = 0

    while time.monotonic() < end:
        it += 1
        y = safe_renormalize(y, S)
        c = fft_conv_self(y)
        score_y = float(c.max())

        if score_y < best - 1e-12:
            best = score_y
            best_a = y.copy()
            last_imp = time.monotonic()
            sf = min(sf_max, sf * 1.05)
            bad = 0
        else:
            if score_y > prev_score + 1e-10:
                bad += 1
            else:
                bad = max(0, bad - 1)
            if bad > 20:
                sf = max(sf_min, sf * 0.85)
                bad = 0
        prev_score = score_y

        target_use = best if target is None else min(best, float(target))
        p = make_weights(c, weight_mode, beta, topk, power, target_use)
        g = gradient_from_weights(y, p)
        gn = float(np.linalg.norm(g))
        if gn < 1e-14:
            break

        rms = float(np.sqrt(np.dot(y, y) / float(n)))
        beta_eff = max(1.0, float(beta))
        if weight_mode == "topk":
            beta_eff = max(5.0, beta_eff)

        step = sf / (beta_eff * gn)
        max_step = 5.0 * rms / max(gn, 1e-15)
        if step > max_step:
            step = max_step

        x = project_simplex(y - step * g, S)

        t_new = 0.5 * (1.0 + np.sqrt(1.0 + 4.0 * t * t))
        y = x + ((t - 1.0) / t_new) * (x - a)
        y = safe_renormalize(y, S)
        a = x
        t = t_new

        now = time.monotonic()
        gap = now - last_imp
        if gap > 60.0:
            sig = max(1e-8, float(noise_scale) * rms)
            y = safe_renormalize(y + rng.normal(0.0, sig, n), S)
            a = y.copy()
            t = 1.0
            sf = max(sf_min, sf * 0.8)

            if gap > 220.0:
                rnd = safe_renormalize(rng.uniform(0.0, 1.0, n), S)
                y = safe_renormalize(0.65 * best_a + 0.35 * rnd, S)
                a = y.copy()
                t = 1.0
                sf = float(step_factor)
                last_imp = now

        if it % 2000 == 0:
            ca = fft_conv_self(a)
            sca = float(ca.max())
            if sca < best - 1e-12:
                best = sca
                best_a = a.copy()
                last_imp = now

    for cand in (best_a, a, y):
        if cand is None or cand.size != n:
            continue
        cand = safe_renormalize(cand, S)
        sc = float(np.max(fft_conv_self(cand)))
        if sc < best - 1e-13:
            best = sc
            best_a = cand.copy()

    return safe_renormalize(best_a, S), best


def run_line_search(a_init, n, S, max_time, rng,
                    weight_mode="target", beta=8.0, topk=512, power=1.5,
                    target=None, momentum=0.2):
    n = int(n)
    S = float(S)
    max_time = float(max_time)
    if max_time <= 0.0 or n <= 0:
        a = normalize_sum(a_init, S)
        return a, sequence_score(a)

    start = time.monotonic()
    end = start + max_time

    a = normalize_sum(a_init, S)
    c0 = fft_conv_self(a)
    best = float(c0.max())
    best_a = a.copy()
    last_imp = start

    step_mult = 1.0
    trials = (1.0, 0.5, 0.25, 0.125)
    it = 0

    while time.monotonic() < end:
        it += 1
        c = fft_conv_self(a)
        score = float(c.max())

        if score < best - 1e-12:
            best = score
            best_a = a.copy()
            last_imp = time.monotonic()

        target_use = best if target is None else min(best, float(target))
        p = make_weights(c, weight_mode, beta, topk, power, target_use)
        g = gradient_from_weights(a, p)
        gn = float(np.linalg.norm(g))
        if gn < 1e-14:
            break

        base_step = 1.0 / gn
        step = base_step * step_mult

        best_x = a
        best_s = score
        best_m = 0.0

        for m in trials:
            x = project_simplex(a - step * m * g, S)
            cx = fft_conv_self(x)
            sx = float(cx.max())
            if sx < best_s - 1e-14:
                best_s = sx
                best_x = x
                best_m = m

        old_a = a
        if best_s < score - 1e-12:
            if momentum > 0.0 and it % 5 == 0:
                diff = best_x - old_a
                x2 = project_simplex(best_x + float(momentum) * diff, S)
                cx2 = fft_conv_self(x2)
                sx2 = float(cx2.max())
                if sx2 < best_s - 1e-14:
                    best_x = x2
                    best_s = sx2

            a = safe_renormalize(best_x, S)
            step_mult = min(4.0, max(0.05, step_mult * max(best_m, 1e-3) * 1.05))
        else:
            if best_x is not a and best_s < score:
                a = safe_renormalize(best_x, S)
            else:
                rms = float(np.sqrt(np.dot(a, a) / float(n)))
                sig = max(1e-8, 0.002 * rms)
                a = safe_renormalize(a + rng.normal(0.0, sig, n), S)
            step_mult = max(0.02, step_mult * 0.5)

        gap = time.monotonic() - last_imp
        if gap > 90.0:
            rnd = safe_renormalize(rng.uniform(0.0, 1.0, n), S)
            a = safe_renormalize(0.8 * best_a + 0.2 * rnd, S)
            step_mult = 1.0
            last_imp = time.monotonic()

    for cand in (best_a, a):
        if cand is None or cand.size != n:
            continue
        cand = safe_renormalize(cand, S)
        sc = float(np.max(fft_conv_self(cand)))
        if sc < best - 1e-13:
            best = sc
            best_a = cand.copy()

    return safe_renormalize(best_a, S), best


def make_basis(n, D):
    n = int(n)
    D = max(1, int(D))
    x = (np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / float(n)
    xs = x - 0.5

    poly_deg = min(6, max(1, D // 4))
    freq = max(1, (D - poly_deg) // 2)
    freq = min(freq, 12)

    cols = []
    for m in range(1, freq + 1):
        cols.append(np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * m * x))
        cols.append(np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * m * x))

    deg = 1
    while len(cols) < D:
        cols.append(xs ** deg)
        deg += 1

    X = np.column_stack(cols)[:, :D]
    X = X - X.mean(axis=0, keepdims=True)
    for j in range(X.shape[1]):
        sd = float(np.std(X[:, j]))
        if sd > 1e-10:
            X[:, j] = X[:, j] / sd
    return X


def basis_to_seq(theta, basis, n, S):
    theta = np.asarray(theta, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    z = basis @ theta
    z = z - float(z.max())
    e = np.exp(z)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
        q = np.full(n, 1.0 / float(n), dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        q = e / s
    return q * float(S)


def run_basis_search(n, S, max_time, rng, D=16, beta=12.0, topk=256,
                     target=None, restarts=60):
    n = int(n)
    S = float(S)
    max_time = float(max_time)
    if n < 32 or max_time <= 0.0:
        a0 = np.full(max(1, n), max(S / max(1, n), 1e-12), dtype=np.float64)
        return a0, sequence_score(a0)

    basis = make_basis(n, D)
    a0 = np.full(n, S / float(n), dtype=np.float64)
    best = float(np.max(fft_conv_self(a0)))
    best_a = a0.copy()

    start = time.monotonic()
    end = start + max_time
    r = 0

    while r < restarts:
        remaining = end - time.monotonic()
        if remaining < 2.0:
            break
        per = max(2.0, min(8.0, remaining / float(max(1, restarts - r))))
        if end - time.monotonic() < per:
            per = max(1.0, end - time.monotonic())

        theta = rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, D)
        mom = np.zeros(D, dtype=np.float64)
        var = np.zeros(D, dtype=np.float64)
        restart_start = time.monotonic()
        last_best = restart_start

        while time.monotonic() < end and (time.monotonic() - restart_start) < per:
            tn = float(np.linalg.norm(theta))
            if tn > 8.0:
                theta = theta * (8.0 / tn)

            a = basis_to_seq(theta, basis, n, S)
            c = fft_conv_self(a)
            score = float(c.max())

            if score < best - 1e-12:
                best = score
                best_a = a.copy()
                last_best = time.monotonic()

            if time.monotonic() - last_best > 3.0:
                break

            target_use = best if target is None else min(best, float(target))
            p = make_weights(c, "mix", beta, min(128, 2 * n - 1), 1.5, target_use)
            ga = gradient_from_weights(a, p)

            q = a / S
            weighted = q * ga
            sumqga = float(np.sum(weighted))
            mean_phi = basis.T @ q
            wmean = basis.T @ weighted
            grad_theta = S * (wmean - mean_phi * sumqga)

            if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad_theta)):
                break

            lr = 0.05
            mom = 0.9 * mom + 0.1 * grad_theta
            var = 0.999 * var + 0.001 * (grad_theta * grad_theta)
            theta = theta - lr * mom / (np.sqrt(var) + 1e-8)

        r += 1

    return best_a, best


def generate_profiles(n, rng):
    n = int(n)
    x = (np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / float(n)
    ax = 2.0 * np.pi * x
    out = []

    out.append(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64))
    out.append(0.5 * (1.0 + np.cos(ax)))
    out.append(0.5 * (1.0 - np.cos(ax)))
    out.append(np.sin(np.pi * x) ** 2)
    out.append(np.sin(np.pi * x) ** 4)

    for center, width in ((0.30, 0.08), (0.70, 0.08), (0.25, 0.13), (0.80, 0.13)):
        out.append(np.exp(-((x - center) ** 2) / (2.0 * width * width)))

    for _ in range(12):
        K = int(rng.integers(2, 5))
        pos = rng.uniform(0.05, 0.95, K)
        widths = rng.uniform(0.03, 0.20, K)
        amps = rng.uniform(0.3, 1.0, K)
        v = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        for j in range(K):
            d = np.abs(x - pos[j])
            d = np.minimum(d, 1.0 - d)
            v += amps[j] * np.exp(-(d * d) / (2.0 * widths[j] * widths[j]))
        out.append(v)

    for _ in range(8):
        v = np.full(n, rng.uniform(0.25, 1.0), dtype=np.float64)
        for m in range(1, 5):
            amp = rng.uniform(0.0, 0.35) / float(m)
            phase = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi)
            v += amp * np.cos(m * ax + phase)
        out.append(v)

    for _ in range(8):
        v = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        for m in range(1, 6):
            amp = rng.uniform(-1.0, 1.0) / (float(m) ** 0.8)
            phase = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi)
            v += amp * np.cos(m * ax + phase)
        scale = rng.uniform(0.5, 2.5)
        v = np.exp(scale * v)
        out.append(v)

    for _ in range(4):
        phi = rng.uniform(0.1, 0.9)
        A = rng.uniform(2.0, 8.0)
        v = 1.0 / (1.0 + np.exp(A * (x - phi))) + 0.05
        out.append(v)

    for _ in range(8):
        p = rng.uniform(0.6, 3.0)
        m = int(rng.integers(1, 6))
        lam = rng.uniform(0.3, 1.0)
        w = np.sin(np.pi * x) ** p
        v = w * (1.0 + lam * np.sin(m * ax))
        out.append(v)

    clean = []
    for a in out:
        a = clean_sequence(a)
        if a.size == n and a.sum() > 1e-12:
            clean.append(a)
    return clean


def resample_sequence(a, n_new):
    a = clean_sequence(a)
    n_new = int(n_new)
    if a.size == n_new:
        return a.copy()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.ones(n_new, dtype=np.float64)
    old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, a.size, dtype=np.float64)
    new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n_new, dtype=np.float64)
    return np.interp(new, old, a, dtype=np.float64)


def get_parent_sequence():
    try:
        p = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
        if p is None:
            return None
        a = clean_sequence(p)
        if a.size < 64 or float(a.sum()) <= 1e-12:
            return None
        return a
    except Exception:
        return None


def make_fallback_list():
    n = 256
    return [1.0] * n


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2380.0, *args, **kwargs):
    for key in ("budget_s", "time_budget", "timeout", "budget"):
        if key in kwargs:
            try:
                budget_s = float(kwargs[key])
            except Exception:
                pass

    try:
        budget_s = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        budget_s = 2380.0
    if not np.isfinite(budget_s) or budget_s <= 0.0:
        budget_s = 2380.0
    budget_s = min(max(budget_s, 120.0), 2380.0)

    start = time.monotonic()
    deadline = start + budget_s - 25.0
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    parent = get_parent_sequence()

    if parent is not None and 1024 <= parent.size <= 16384:
        main_n = int(parent.size)
    else:
        main_n = 8192

    S = float(np.sqrt(2.0 * main_n))

    if parent is not None:
        pmain = parent
        if pmain.size != main_n:
            pmain = resample_sequence(pmain, main_n)
        best_a = normalize_sum(pmain, S)
    else:
        best_a = np.full(main_n, S / float(main_n), dtype=np.float64)

    best_score = sequence_score(best_a)
    print("initial n=%d score=%.8f elapsed=%.1fs" % (main_n, best_score, time.monotonic() - start))

    inits = []
    if parent is not None:
        pmain = parent
        if pmain.size != main_n:
            pmain = resample_sequence(pmain, main_n)
        inits.append(pmain)
        inits.append(pmain[::-1])
        inits.append(0.5 * (pmain + pmain[::-1]))

    inits.extend(generate_profiles(main_n, rng))
    inits.append(rng.uniform(0.0, 1.0, main_n))

    scored = []
    for idx, arr in enumerate(inits):
        a = normalize_sum(arr, S)
        if a.size != main_n:
            a = resample_sequence(a, main_n)
            a = normalize_sum(a, S)
        sc = sequence_score(a)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            scored.append((sc, idx, a))
    scored.sort()

    for sc, idx, a in scored:
        if sc < best_score - 1e-13:
            best_score = sc
            best_a = a.copy()
            print("better init score=%.8f elapsed=%.1fs" % (best_score, time.monotonic() - start))

    task_arrays = [best_a.copy()]
    for sc, idx, a in scored:
        if len(task_arrays) >= 6:
            break
        task_arrays.append(a.copy())

    # Coarse basis construction.
    remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if remaining > 450.0:
        n_coarse = max(256, min(1024, main_n // 8))
        S_coarse = float(np.sqrt(2.0 * n_coarse))
        coarse_time = min(280.0, remaining * 0.25)
        print("coarse basis n=%d time=%.0fs" % (n_coarse, coarse_time))
        coarse_a, coarse_sc = run_basis_search(n_coarse, S_coarse, coarse_time, rng,
                                                D=16, beta=12.0, topk=256, target=TARGET_UPPER)
        if coarse_a is not None and coarse_a.size == n_coarse:
            a_up = normalize_sum(resample_sequence(coarse_a, main_n), S)
            sc_up = sequence_score(a_up)
            if sc_up < best_score - 1e-13:
                best_score = sc_up
                best_a = a_up.copy()
                print("coarse-upscored score=%.8f elapsed=%.1fs" % (best_score, time.monotonic() - start))
            task_arrays.append(a_up.copy())

    remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if remaining > 420.0 and main_n <= 16384:
        basis_time = min(110.0, remaining * 0.12)
        print("fine basis n=%d time=%.0fs" % (main_n, basis_time))
        basis_a, basis_sc = run_basis_search(main_n, S, basis_time, rng,
                                             D=22, beta=12.0, topk=512, target=TARGET_UPPER)
        if basis_a is not None and basis_a.size == main_n:
            basis_a = normalize_sum(basis_a, S)
            sc_basis = sequence_score(basis_a)
            if sc_basis < best_score - 1e-13:
                best_score = sc_basis
                best_a = basis_a.copy()
                print("basis score=%.8f elapsed=%.1fs" % (best_score, time.monotonic() - start))
            else:
                task_arrays.append(basis_a.copy())

    # Main full-dimensional local search.
    remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
    main_budget = min(1200.0, max(80.0, remaining * 0.55))
    print("main local search budget=%.0fs best=%.8f" % (main_budget, best_score))

    settings = [
        {"weight_mode": "target", "beta": 8.0, "topk": 1024, "power": 1.0, "step_factor": 2.0},
        {"weight_mode": "topk", "beta": 20.0, "topk": 2048, "power": 1.0, "step_factor": 1.3},
        {"weight_mode": "mix", "beta": 30.0, "topk": 1024, "power": 1.5, "step_factor": 1.0},
        {"weight_mode": "lse", "beta": 50.0, "topk": 512, "power": 1.0, "step_factor": 0.9},
        {"weight_mode": "target", "beta": 20.0, "topk": 1024, "power": 1.0, "step_factor": 2.5},
        {"weight_mode": "topk", "beta": 40.0, "topk": 256, "power": 2.0, "step_factor": 1.1},
    ]

    tasks = []
    tasks.append((task_arrays[0], settings[0], 0.28))
    if len(task_arrays) > 1:
        tasks.append((task_arrays[1], settings[1], 0.18))
    if len(task_arrays) > 2:
        tasks.append((task_arrays[2], settings[2], 0.12))
    tasks.append((best_a.copy(), settings[3], 0.12))
    if len(task_arrays) > 1:
        tasks.append((task_arrays[1].copy(), settings[4], 0.10))
    if len(task_arrays) > 3:
        tasks.append((task_arrays[3].copy(), settings[5], 0.10))
    else:
        tasks.append((best_a.copy(), settings[5], 0.10))

    total_w = float(sum(w for _, _, w in tasks))
    for arr, st, w in tasks:
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 20.0:
            break
        t = main_budget * (float(w) / total_w)
        if t < 8.0:
            continue
        if t > deadline - time.monotonic() - 15.0:
            t = max(1.0, deadline - time.monotonic() - 15.0)

        a_opt, sc_opt = run_fista(arr, main_n, S, t, rng, target=TARGET_UPPER, **st)
        a_opt = normalize_sum(a_opt, S)
        sc_opt = sequence_score(a_opt)
        if sc_opt < best_score - 1e-13:
            best_score = sc_opt
            best_a = a_opt.copy()
            print("main task improved score=%.8f elapsed=%.1fs" % (best_score, time.monotonic() - start))

    # Line-search polishing.
    remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
    line_budget = min(520.0, max(30.0, remaining * 0.42))
    if line_budget > 25.0 and time.monotonic() < deadline - 25.0:
        print("line polish budget=%.0fs best=%.8f" % (line_budget, best_score))
        a_opt, sc_opt = run_line_search(best_a, main_n, S, line_budget, rng,
                                        weight_mode="target", beta=8.0,
                                        topk=512, power=1.5, target=TARGET_UPPER)
        a_opt = normalize_sum(a_opt, S)
        sc_opt = sequence_score(a_opt)
        if sc_opt < best_score - 1e-13:
            best_score = sc_opt
            best_a = a_opt.copy()
            print("line polish improved score=%.8f elapsed=%.1fs" % (best_score, time.monotonic() - start))

    remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
    micro_budget = min(160.0, max(10.0, remaining * 0.22))
    if micro_budget > 10.0 and time.monotonic() < deadline - 20.0:
        a_opt, sc_opt = run_fista(best_a, main_n, S, micro_budget, rng,
                                  weight_mode="target", beta=30.0, topk=256,
                                  power=2.0, step_factor=2.5, target=TARGET_UPPER)
        a_opt = normalize_sum(a_opt, S)
        sc_opt = sequence_score(a_opt)
        if sc_opt < best_score - 1e-13:
            best_score = sc_opt
            best_a = a_opt.copy()
            print("micro target improved score=%.8f elapsed=%.1fs" % (best_score, time.monotonic() - start))

    # Optional second length.
    remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if remaining > 320.0:
        if main_n < 16384:
            alt_n = 16384
        else:
            alt_n = max(4096, main_n // 2)
        if 1024 <= alt_n <= 16384 and alt_n != main_n:
            S_alt = float(np.sqrt(2.0 * alt_n))
            alt_a = normalize_sum(resample_sequence(best_a, alt_n), S_alt)
            alt_score = sequence_score(alt_a)
            if alt_score < best_score - 1e-13:
                best_score = alt_score
                best_a = alt_a.copy()

            alt_total = min(360.0, remaining * 0.35)
            t1 = max(1.0, alt_total * 0.45)
            a_opt, sc_opt = run_fista(alt_a, alt_n, S_alt, t1, rng,
                                      weight_mode="target", beta=8.0,
                                      topk=1024, power=1.0, step_factor=2.0,
                                      target=TARGET_UPPER)
            a_opt = normalize_sum(a_opt, S_alt)
            sc_opt = sequence_score(a_opt)
            if sc_opt < best_score - 1e-13:
                best_score = sc_opt
                best_a = a_opt.copy()
                print("alt length n=%d score=%.8f" % (alt_n, best_score))

            remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
            t2 = min(120.0, max(1.0, remaining * 0.35))
            if t2 > 10.0:
                a_opt, sc_opt = run_line_search(best_a, best_a.size,
                                                float(np.sqrt(2.0 * best_a.size)),
                                                t2, rng,
                                                weight_mode="target", beta=10.0,
                                                topk=512, power=1.5, target=TARGET_UPPER)
                a_opt = normalize_sum(a_opt, best_a.size)
                sc_opt = sequence_score(a_opt)
                if sc_opt < best_score - 1e-13:
                    best_score = sc_opt
                    best_a = a_opt.copy()
                    print("alt line n=%d score=%.8f" % (best_a.size, best_score))

    remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if remaining > 25.0:
        a_opt, sc_opt = run_line_search(best_a, best_a.size,
                                        float(np.sqrt(2.0 * best_a.size)),
                                        min(20.0, remaining - 10.0), rng,
                                        weight_mode="target", beta=15.0,
                                        topk=256, power=1.5, target=TARGET_UPPER)
        a_opt = normalize_sum(a_opt, best_a.size)
        sc_opt = sequence_score(a_opt)
        if sc_opt < best_score - 1e-13:
            best_score = sc_opt
            best_a = a_opt.copy()

    best_a = clean_sequence(best_a)
    if best_a.size == 0 or best_a.sum() <= 1e-12:
        return make_fallback_list()

    best_a = normalize_sum(best_a, None)
    final_list = [float(x) for x in best_a]

    eval_func = globals().get("evaluate_sequence", None)
    if eval_func is not None and time.monotonic() < deadline - 2.0:
        try:
            v = eval_func(final_list)
            print("final internal score=%.8f evaluator=%.8f len=%d" %
                  (best_score, float(v) if np.isfinite(v) else np.inf, len(final_list)))
            if not np.isfinite(v):
                return make_fallback_list()
        except Exception:
            pass

    return final_list
```
